                    description=failure_log[-500:],
                    status="open",
                    cycle=test_sequence,
                    evidence={"returncode": test_result["returncode"], "log": test_result["log"]},
                )
                ledger.record_event(
                    phase="test",
                    action=f"test_{test_sequence}",
                    result="failed",
                    details=test_result,
                    issue_links=[test_issue],
                    artifacts=[f"test_{test_sequence}.log"],
                )

            debug_attempt = 0
            while not test_result["passed"] and debug_attempt < config.max_debug_attempts:
                debug_attempt += 1
                current_diff = git(worktree, "diff", "--binary", "HEAD", "--").stdout
                failure_log = Path(test_result["log"]).read_text(encoding="utf-8")[-12_000:]
                diagnosis = self._luna_repair(
                    run_dir=run_dir,
                    config=config,
                    plan=plan,
                    diff_text=current_diff,
                    failure=failure_log,
                    label=f"luna_debug_{debug_attempt}",
                )
                luna_issue = ledger.record_or_update_issue(
                    source="Luna Debugger",
                    title=f"Luna diagnosis {debug_attempt}: {diagnosis['root_cause'][:120]}",
                    description=diagnosis["root_cause"],
                    status="open",
                    cycle=debug_attempt,
                    evidence={"repair_steps": diagnosis["repair_steps"], "files": diagnosis["files"]},
                )
                ledger.record_event(
                    phase="luna_debug",
                    action=f"luna_debug_{debug_attempt}",
                    result="success",
                    objective_class="repair",
                    details={"root_cause": diagnosis["root_cause"]},
                    issue_links=[luna_issue],
                    artifacts=[f"luna_debug_{debug_attempt}.json"],
                )
                instruction = (
                    "Apply Luna's approved repair diagnosis without redesigning the solution. Hard file boundary remains unchanged.\n\n"
                    + json.dumps(diagnosis, indent=2)
                )
                self.builder.run(
                    worktree=worktree,
                    instruction=instruction,
                    allowed_files=allowed,
                    config=config,
                    run_dir=run_dir,
                    label=f"builder_debug_{debug_attempt}",
                )
                ledger.record_event(
                    phase="build",
                    action=f"builder_debug_{debug_attempt}",
                    result="success",
                    objective_class="repair",
                    issue_links=[luna_issue],
                    artifacts=self._builder_artifacts(run_dir, f"builder_debug_{debug_attempt}"),
                )
                self._assert_source_unchanged(
                    Path(config.repo), state, run_dir, f"after_builder_debug_{debug_attempt}"
                )
                self._scope_check(worktree, allowed, f"after_debug_{debug_attempt}", run_dir)
                test_sequence += 1
                test_result = self._test(worktree, config, run_dir, test_sequence)
                tests.append(test_result)
                self._assert_source_unchanged(
                    Path(config.repo), state, run_dir, f"after_test_{test_sequence}"
                )
                self._scope_check(worktree, allowed, f"after_test_{test_sequence}", run_dir)
                if test_result["passed"]:
                    self._create_checkpoint(worktree, run_dir, f"test_pass_{test_sequence}")
                    checkpoints.append(f"test_pass_{test_sequence}")
                    ledger.resolve_issues_by_source(
                        "Test Runner",
                        resolution_evidence={"test_sequence": test_sequence, "passed": True},
                    )
                    ledger.resolve_issues_by_source(
                        "Luna Debugger",
                        resolution_evidence={"test_sequence": test_sequence, "passed": True},
                    )
                    ledger.record_event(
                        phase="test",
                        action=f"test_{test_sequence}",
                        result="success",
                        details=test_result,
                        artifacts=[f"test_{test_sequence}.log"],
                    )
                else:
                    failure_log = Path(test_result["log"]).read_text(encoding="utf-8")[-12_000:]
                    test_issue = ledger.record_or_update_issue(
                        source="Test Runner",
                        title=f"Test sequence {test_sequence} failed",
                        description=failure_log[-500:],
                        status="open",
                        cycle=test_sequence,
                        evidence={"returncode": test_result["returncode"], "log": test_result["log"]},
                    )
                    ledger.record_event(
                        phase="test",
                        action=f"test_{test_sequence}",
                        result="failed",
                        details=test_result,
                        issue_links=[test_issue],
                        artifacts=[f"test_{test_sequence}.log"],
                    )

            if not test_result["passed"]:
                raise FactoryError("tests still fail after Luna repair budget", "test")

            review_sequence = 1
            while review_sequence <= config.max_review_cycles + 1:
                diff_text, digest, changed = self._stage(worktree, allowed, run_dir, review_sequence)
                # Deterministic Pre-Review Gate before calling Sol Reviewer
                gate_record = self._pre_review_gate(worktree, allowed, changed, diff_text, tests, run_dir, review_sequence)
                ledger.record_event(
                    phase="gate",
                    action=f"pre_review_gate_{review_sequence}",
                    result="success",
                    details=gate_record.get("checks"),
                    artifacts=[f"pre_review_gate_{review_sequence}.json"],
                )

                candidate_source_context = self._candidate_source_context(
                    worktree, allowed, changed, run_dir, review_sequence
                )
                self._assert_source_unchanged(
                    Path(config.repo), state, run_dir, f"before_review_{review_sequence}"
                )
                review = self._review(
                    run_dir=run_dir,
                    config=config,
                    plan=plan,
                    diff_text=diff_text,
                    digest=digest,
                    changed=changed,
                    candidate_source_context=candidate_source_context,
                    tests=tests,
                    sequence=review_sequence,
                )
                if review["decision"] == "APPROVE":
                    self._assert_source_unchanged(
                        Path(config.repo), state, run_dir, f"commit_gate_{review_sequence}"
                    )
                    self._scope_check(worktree, allowed, f"commit_gate_{review_sequence}", run_dir)
                    current_diff = git(worktree, "diff", "--cached", "--binary", "--no-ext-diff").stdout
                    current_hash = hashlib.sha256(current_diff.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest()
                    if current_hash != digest or review["reviewed_diff_sha256"] != current_hash:
                        raise FactoryError("candidate changed after Sol approval; approval invalidated", "hash")
                    message = (
                        f"hermes: {plan['objective'].strip()[:68]}\n\n"
                        f"Factory-Run: {identifier}\n"
                        f"Sol-Model: {config.sol_model}\n"
                        f"Luna-Model: {config.luna_model}\n"
                        f"Builder-Model: {config.builder_model}\n"
                        f"Reviewed-Diff-SHA256: {current_hash}"
                    )
                    committed = command(["git", "commit", "-m", message], cwd=worktree, timeout=120)
                    if committed.returncode != 0:
                        raise FactoryError(f"commit failed: {(committed.stderr or committed.stdout).strip()}", "git")
                    commit_hash = git(worktree, "rev-parse", "HEAD").stdout.strip()
                    ledger.resolve_issues_by_source(
                        "Sol Reviewer",
                        resolution_evidence={"reviewed_diff_sha256": current_hash, "decision": "APPROVE"},
                    )
                    ledger.record_event(
                        phase="review",
                        action=f"sol_review_{review_sequence}",
                        result="approved",
                        details={"decision": "APPROVE", "reviewed_diff_sha256": current_hash},
                        artifacts=[f"sol_review_{review_sequence}.json"],
                    )
                    ledger.record_event(
                        phase="commit",
                        action="git_commit",
                        result="success",
                        details={"commit": commit_hash, "reviewed_diff_sha256": current_hash},
                    )
                    self._save(
                        run_dir,
                        state,
                        "committed",
                        commit=commit_hash,
                        reviewed_diff_sha256=current_hash,
                        changed_files=changed,
                        test_results=tests,
                        review_cycles=review_sequence,
                        checkpoints=checkpoints,
                    )
                    # The branch is retained. The now-clean worktree is removed to avoid accumulation.
                    removed = command(["git", "worktree", "remove", str(worktree)], cwd=Path(config.repo), timeout=120)
                    if removed.returncode == 0:
                        state["worktree_removed"] = True
                        atomic_json(run_dir / "run_state.json", state)
                    return state

                if review_sequence > config.max_review_cycles:
                    ledger.record_event(
                        phase="review",
                        action=f"sol_review_{review_sequence}",
                        result="rejected",
                        details={"decision": "REJECT", "findings": review.get("findings", [])},
                    )
                    raise FactoryError("Sol rejected candidate after review rework budget", "review")

                # Record checkpoint before rework and reset staged changes
                cp_rework = self._create_checkpoint(worktree, run_dir, f"rejection_{review_sequence}")
                checkpoints.append(f"rejection_{review_sequence}")
                git(worktree, "reset", "--quiet")
                rev_issue_ids: list[str] = []
                for f_idx, finding in enumerate(review.get("findings", [])):
                    iss_id = ledger.record_or_update_issue(
                        source="Sol Reviewer",
                        title=f"Sol review {review_sequence} finding: {finding[:100]}",
                        description=finding,
                        status="open",
                        severity="rejection",
                        cycle=review_sequence,
                        evidence={"required_changes": review.get("required_changes", [])},
                    )
                    rev_issue_ids.append(iss_id)
                ledger.record_event(
                    phase="review",
                    action=f"sol_review_{review_sequence}",
                    result="rejected",
                    details={
                        "decision": "REJECT",
                        "findings": review.get("findings", []),
                        "route": review.get("route"),
                    },
                    issue_links=rev_issue_ids,
                    artifacts=[f"sol_review_{review_sequence}.json"],
                )
                required = "\n".join(f"- {item}" for item in review["required_changes"])
                if review["route"] == "luna":
                    diagnosis = self._luna_repair(
                        run_dir=run_dir,
                        config=config,
                        plan=plan,
                        diff_text=diff_text,
                        failure="Sol review rejection:\n" + required,
                        label=f"luna_review_rework_{review_sequence}",
                    )
                    luna_rev_issue = ledger.record_or_update_issue(
                        source="Luna Debugger",
                        title=f"Luna review diagnosis {review_sequence}: {diagnosis['root_cause'][:120]}",
                        description=diagnosis["root_cause"],
                        status="open",
                        cycle=review_sequence,
                        evidence={"repair_steps": diagnosis["repair_steps"], "files": diagnosis["files"]},
                    )
                    ledger.record_event(
                        phase="luna_debug",
                        action=f"luna_review_rework_{review_sequence}",
                        result="success",
                        objective_class="repair",
                        details={"root_cause": diagnosis["root_cause"]},
                        issue_links=[luna_rev_issue],
                        artifacts=[f"luna_review_rework_{review_sequence}.json"],
                    )
                    rework = "Apply Luna's repair plan for the rejected candidate:\n" + json.dumps(diagnosis, indent=2)
                else:
                    rework = "Sol rejected the candidate. Apply only these required changes:\n" + required
                self.builder.run(
                    worktree=worktree,
                    instruction=rework,
                    allowed_files=allowed,
                    config=config,
                    run_dir=run_dir,
                    label=f"builder_review_rework_{review_sequence}",
                )
                ledger.record_event(
                    phase="build",
                    action=f"builder_review_rework_{review_sequence}",
                    result="success",
                    objective_class="repair",
                    issue_links=rev_issue_ids,
                    artifacts=self._builder_artifacts(run_dir, f"builder_review_rework_{review_sequence}"),
                )
                self._assert_source_unchanged(
                    Path(config.repo), state, run_dir, f"after_builder_review_rework_{review_sequence}"
                )
                self._scope_check(worktree, allowed, f"after_review_rework_{review_sequence}", run_dir)
                test_sequence += 1
                test_result = self._test(worktree, config, run_dir, test_sequence)
                tests.append(test_result)
                self._assert_source_unchanged(
                    Path(config.repo), state, run_dir, f"after_test_{test_sequence}"
                )
                self._scope_check(worktree, allowed, f"after_test_{test_sequence}", run_dir)
                if test_result["passed"]:
                    self._create_checkpoint(worktree, run_dir, f"test_pass_{test_sequence}")
                    checkpoints.append(f"test_pass_{test_sequence}")
                    ledger.resolve_issues_by_source(
                        "Test Runner",
                        resolution_evidence={"test_sequence": test_sequence, "passed": True},
                    )
                    ledger.resolve_issues_by_source(
                        "Luna Debugger",
                        resolution_evidence={"test_sequence": test_sequence, "passed": True},
                    )
                    ledger.record_event(
                        phase="test",
                        action=f"test_{test_sequence}",
                        result="success",
                        details=test_result,
                        artifacts=[f"test_{test_sequence}.log"],
                    )
                else:
                    failure_log = Path(test_result["log"]).read_text(encoding="utf-8")[-12_000:]
                    test_issue = ledger.record_or_update_issue(
                        source="Test Runner",
                        title=f"Test sequence {test_sequence} failed",
                        description=failure_log[-500:],
                        status="open",
                        cycle=test_sequence,
                        evidence={"returncode": test_result["returncode"], "log": test_result["log"]},
                    )
                    ledger.record_event(
                        phase="test",
                        action=f"test_{test_sequence}",
                        result="failed",
                        details=test_result,
                        issue_links=[test_issue],
                        artifacts=[f"test_{test_sequence}.log"],
                    )

                while not test_result["passed"] and debug_attempt < config.max_debug_attempts:
                    debug_attempt += 1
                    current_diff = git(worktree, "diff", "--binary", "HEAD", "--").stdout
                    failure_log = Path(test_result["log"]).read_text(encoding="utf-8")[-12_000:]
                    diagnosis = self._luna_repair(
                        run_dir=run_dir,
                        config=config,
                        plan=plan,
                        diff_text=current_diff,
                        failure=failure_log,
                        label=f"luna_review_debug_{review_sequence}_{debug_attempt}",
                    )
                    luna_issue = ledger.record_or_update_issue(
                        source="Luna Debugger",
                        title=f"Luna review debug {review_sequence}_{debug_attempt}: {diagnosis['root_cause'][:120]}",
                        description=diagnosis["root_cause"],
                        status="open",
                        cycle=f"{review_sequence}_{debug_attempt}",
                        evidence={"repair_steps": diagnosis["repair_steps"], "files": diagnosis["files"]},
                    )
                    ledger.record_event(
                        phase="luna_debug",
                        action=f"luna_review_debug_{review_sequence}_{debug_attempt}",
                        result="success",
                        objective_class="repair",
                        details={"root_cause": diagnosis["root_cause"]},
                        issue_links=[luna_issue],
                        artifacts=[f"luna_review_debug_{review_sequence}_{debug_attempt}.json"],
                    )
                    self.builder.run(
                        worktree=worktree,
                        instruction=(
                            "The reviewer-requested rework failed tests. Apply Luna's diagnosis within the unchanged file boundary:\n"
                            + json.dumps(diagnosis, indent=2)
                        ),
                        allowed_files=allowed,
                        config=config,
                        run_dir=run_dir,
                        label=f"builder_review_debug_{review_sequence}_{debug_attempt}",
                    )
                    ledger.record_event(
                        phase="build",
                        action=f"builder_review_debug_{review_sequence}_{debug_attempt}",
                        result="success",
                        objective_class="repair",
                        issue_links=[luna_issue],
                        artifacts=self._builder_artifacts(run_dir, f"builder_review_debug_{review_sequence}_{debug_attempt}"),
                    )
                    self._assert_source_unchanged(
                        Path(config.repo),
                        state,
                        run_dir,
                        f"after_builder_review_debug_{review_sequence}_{debug_attempt}",
                    )
                    self._scope_check(
                        worktree,
                        allowed,
                        f"after_review_debug_{review_sequence}_{debug_attempt}",
                        run_dir,
                    )
                    test_sequence += 1
                    test_result = self._test(worktree, config, run_dir, test_sequence)
                    tests.append(test_result)
                    self._assert_source_unchanged(
                        Path(config.repo), state, run_dir, f"after_test_{test_sequence}"
                    )
                    self._scope_check(worktree, allowed, f"after_test_{test_sequence}", run_dir)
                    if test_result["passed"]:
                        self._create_checkpoint(worktree, run_dir, f"test_pass_{test_sequence}")
                        checkpoints.append(f"test_pass_{test_sequence}")
                        ledger.resolve_issues_by_source(
                            "Test Runner",
                            resolution_evidence={"test_sequence": test_sequence, "passed": True},
                        )
                        ledger.resolve_issues_by_source(
                            "Luna Debugger",
                            resolution_evidence={"test_sequence": test_sequence, "passed": True},
                        )
                        ledger.record_event(
                            phase="test",
                            action=f"test_{test_sequence}",
                            result="success",
                            details=test_result,
                            artifacts=[f"test_{test_sequence}.log"],
                        )
                    else:
                        failure_log = Path(test_result["log"]).read_text(encoding="utf-8")[-12_000:]
                        test_issue = ledger.record_or_update_issue(
                            source="Test Runner",
                            title=f"Test sequence {test_sequence} failed",
                            description=failure_log[-500:],
                            status="open",
                            cycle=test_sequence,
                            evidence={"returncode": test_result["returncode"], "log": test_result["log"]},
                        )
                        ledger.record_event(
                            phase="test",
                            action=f"test_{test_sequence}",
                            result="failed",
                            details=test_result,
                            issue_links=[test_issue],
                            artifacts=[f"test_{test_sequence}.log"],
                        )
                if not test_result["passed"]:
                    raise FactoryError("tests failed after reviewer rework and Luna repair budget", "test")
                review_sequence += 1
            raise FactoryError("review loop ended without approval", "review")
        except FactoryError as exc:
            phase = "blocked" if exc.category in {"preflight", "provider", "base"} else "failed"
            ledger.record_event(
                phase=phase,
                action="execution_aborted",
                result=exc.category,
                details={"error": str(exc), "category": exc.category},
            )
            self._save(run_dir, state, phase, error=str(exc), error_category=exc.category, test_results=tests)
            raise

    def status(self, identifier: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return read_run_state(self._run_dir(identifier))

    def resume(self, identifier: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        run_dir, state, config = self._load(identifier)
        if state["phase"] == "committed":
            return state
        if state["phase"] not in {"planned", "blocked", "failed", "worktree_ready"}:
            raise FactoryError(f"run cannot be resumed safely from phase {state['phase']!r}", "state")
        if not (run_dir / "sol_plan.json").is_file():
            repo = self._repo(config.repo)
            try:
                self._perform_planning(run_dir, state, config, repo)
            except FactoryError as exc:
                phase = "blocked" if exc.category in {"preflight", "provider"} else "failed"
                self._save(run_dir, state, phase, error=str(exc), error_category=exc.category)
                raise
        else:
            state["warm_start"] = True
            atomic_json(run_dir / "warm_start.json", {"warm_start": True, "resumed_from": state.get("phase"), "timestamp": now_iso()})
            atomic_json(run_dir / "run_state.json", state)
        return self.execute(identifier)


def split_files(raw_values: list[str] | None) -> list[str] | None:
    if not raw_values:
        return None
    values: list[str] = []
    for raw in raw_values:
        values.extend(item.strip() for item in raw.split(",") if item.strip())
    return normalized_paths(values)


def add_common(parser: argparse.ArgumentParser) -> None:
    parser.add_argument("repo", help="Git repository top-level path")
    parser.add_argument("request", help="Implementation request")
    parser.add_argument("--files", action="append", help="Hard file boundary; repeat or comma-separate")
    parser.add_argument("--test-cmd", default=None, help="Explicit test command")
    parser.add_argument("--prior-run", default=None, help="Prior run ID for progressive disclosure context")
    parser.add_argument("--planner-provider", default="openai-codex")
    parser.add_argument("--planner-model", default=DEFAULT_PLANNER_MODEL)
    parser.add_argument("--planner-reasoning", default=DEFAULT_PLANNER_REASONING)
    parser.add_argument("--reviewer-provider", default="openai-codex")
    parser.add_argument("--reviewer-model", default=DEFAULT_REVIEWER_MODEL)
    parser.add_argument("--reviewer-reasoning", default=DEFAULT_REVIEWER_REASONING)
    parser.add_argument("--sol-provider", default="openai-codex")
    parser.add_argument("--sol-model", default=DEFAULT_SOL_MODEL)
    parser.add_argument("--luna-provider", default="openai-codex")
    parser.add_argument("--luna-model", default=DEFAULT_LUNA_MODEL)
    parser.add_argument("--luna-reasoning", default=DEFAULT_LUNA_REASONING)
    parser.add_argument("--builder-model", default=DEFAULT_BUILDER_MODEL)
    parser.add_argument("--agy-bin", default=None)
    parser.add_argument("--max-debug-attempts", type=int, default=2)
    parser.add_argument("--max-review-cycles", type=int, default=2)
    parser.add_argument("--role-timeout", type=int, default=300)
    parser.add_argument("--builder-timeout", type=int, default=900)
    parser.add_argument("--test-timeout", type=int, default=900)


def config_from_args(args: argparse.Namespace) -> Config:
    return Config(
        repo=args.repo,
        request=args.request,
        requested_files=split_files(args.files),
        test_command=args.test_cmd,
        prior_run=getattr(args, "prior_run", None),
        planner_provider=args.planner_provider,
        planner_model=args.planner_model,
        planner_reasoning=args.planner_reasoning,
        reviewer_provider=args.reviewer_provider,
        reviewer_model=args.reviewer_model,
        reviewer_reasoning=args.reviewer_reasoning,
        sol_provider=args.sol_provider,
        sol_model=args.sol_model,
        luna_provider=args.luna_provider,
        luna_model=args.luna_model,
        luna_reasoning=args.luna_reasoning,
        builder_model=args.builder_model,
        agy_bin=getattr(args, "agy_bin", None),
        max_debug_attempts=args.max_debug_attempts,
        max_review_cycles=args.max_review_cycles,
        role_timeout=args.role_timeout,
        builder_timeout=args.builder_timeout,
        test_timeout=args.test_timeout,
    )


def print_result(state: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    print(json.dumps(state, indent=2, sort_keys=True))


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Hermes Software Factory v2")
    parser.add_argument("--state-root", default=str(DEFAULT_STATE_ROOT), help="Run artifact root")
    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
    run_parser = sub.add_parser("run", help="plan and execute a complete task")
    add_common(run_parser)
    plan_parser = sub.add_parser("plan", help="create and validate a Sol plan only")
    add_common(plan_parser)
    for name in ("execute", "resume", "status"):
        child = sub.add_parser(name)
        child.add_argument("run_id")
    sub.choices["status"].add_argument("--json", action="store_true", help="print full JSON")

    args = parser.parse_args(argv)
    engine = FactoryEngine(state_root=Path(args.state_root))
    try:
        if args.command == "plan":
            identifier = engine.plan(config_from_args(args))
            print(identifier)
        elif args.command == "run":
            identifier = engine.plan(config_from_args(args))
            print(f"Run: {identifier}", file=sys.stderr)
            print_result(engine.execute(identifier))
        elif args.command == "execute":
            print_result(engine.execute(args.run_id))
        elif args.command == "resume":
            print_result(engine.resume(args.run_id))
        else:
            state = engine.status(args.run_id)
            if args.json:
                print_result(state)
            else:
                summary = {key: state.get(key) for key in ("run_id", "phase", "branch", "commit", "error") if state.get(key) is not None}
                print_result(summary)
        return 0
    except FactoryError as exc:
        print(f"Factory {exc.category}: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
"""Step 5 tests for AGY executable resolution and capability preflight."""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import os
import subprocess
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path
from unittest.mock import patch

import sys

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "lib"))

import hermes_factory_v2 as factory_module

from hermes_factory_v2 import (
    AGY_REQUIRED_CAPABILITIES,
    AgyBuilder,
    Config,
    FactoryError,
    FactoryEngine,
    atomic_json,
    resolve_agy_executable,
)


HELP_TEXT = "\n".join(
    [
        "Usage of agy.exe:",
        *(
            (
                f"  {capability}                 "
                "Output format in print mode (text, json, stream-json)"
                if capability == "--output-format"
                else f"  {capability}"
            )
            for capability in AGY_REQUIRED_CAPABILITIES
        ),
    ]
)
MODEL_TEXT = (
    "Fetching available models...\n"
    "gemini-3.8-flash-high\tGemini 3.8 Flash (High)\n"
    "gemini-3.1-pro-high\tGemini 3.1 Pro (High)\n"
)


class CommandFixture:
    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        model: str,
        version: str = "1.2.0",
        help_text: str = HELP_TEXT,
        models_text: str = MODEL_TEXT,
        probe_stdout: str | None = None,
        probe_returncode: int = 0,
        probe_stderr: str = "",
        probe_model: str | None = None,
        probe_effective_cwd: Path | None = None,
        probe_result_conversation_id: str | None = None,
        probe_duplicate_result: bool = False,
        probe_order: str | None = None,
        probe_create_file: bool = False,
        probe_create_parent_file: bool = False,
        probe_tools: list[object] | None = None,
        probe_permission_mode: str = "always-proceed",
        probe_num_turns: int = 1,
        probe_duration_seconds: object = 0.1,
        probe_drop_result_keys: tuple[str, ...] = (),
    ) -> None:
        self.model = model
        self.version = version
        self.help_text = help_text
        self.models_text = models_text
        self.probe_stdout = probe_stdout
        self.probe_returncode = probe_returncode
        self.probe_stderr = probe_stderr
        self.probe_model = probe_model or model
        self.probe_effective_cwd = probe_effective_cwd
        self.probe_result_conversation_id = probe_result_conversation_id
        self.probe_duplicate_result = probe_duplicate_result
        self.probe_order = probe_order
        self.probe_create_file = probe_create_file
        self.probe_create_parent_file = probe_create_parent_file
        self.probe_tools = probe_tools
        self.probe_permission_mode = probe_permission_mode
        self.probe_num_turns = probe_num_turns
        self.probe_duration_seconds = probe_duration_seconds
        self.probe_drop_result_keys = probe_drop_result_keys
        self.calls: list[tuple[list[str], dict[str, object]]] = []

    def __call__(self, argv: list[str], **kwargs: object) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]:
        self.calls.append((list(argv), kwargs))
        if len(argv) > 1 and argv[1] == "--version":
            return subprocess.CompletedProcess(argv, 0, self.version + "\n", "")
        if len(argv) > 1 and argv[1] == "--help":
            return subprocess.CompletedProcess(argv, 0, self.help_text, "")
        if len(argv) > 1 and argv[1] == "models":
            return subprocess.CompletedProcess(argv, 0, self.models_text, "")
        if self.probe_stdout is None:
            probe_dir = Path(str(kwargs["cwd"])).resolve()
            self.probe_stdout = make_probe_stream(
                probe_dir,
                self.probe_model,
                effective_cwd=self.probe_effective_cwd,
                result_conversation_id=self.probe_result_conversation_id,
                duplicate_result=self.probe_duplicate_result,
                tools=self.probe_tools,
                permission_mode=self.probe_permission_mode,
                num_turns=self.probe_num_turns,
                duration_seconds=self.probe_duration_seconds,
                drop_result_keys=self.probe_drop_result_keys,
            )
            if self.probe_order:
                events = [json.loads(line) for line in self.probe_stdout.splitlines() if line.strip()]
                if self.probe_order == "result_before_init":
                    events = [events[-1], *events[:-1]]
                elif self.probe_order == "event_after_result":
                    events = [events[0], events[-1], *events[1:-1]]
                else:
                    raise AssertionError(f"unknown probe order: {self.probe_order}")
                self.probe_stdout = "\n".join(json.dumps(event) for event in events) + "\n"
            if self.probe_create_file:
                (probe_dir / "unexpected.txt").write_text("probe wrote a file\n", encoding="utf-8")
            if self.probe_create_parent_file:
                (probe_dir.parent.parent / "escaped.txt").write_text(
                    "probe escaped its private root\n",
                    encoding="utf-8",
                )
        return subprocess.CompletedProcess(
            argv,
            self.probe_returncode,
            self.probe_stdout,
            self.probe_stderr,
        )


def make_probe_stream(
    probe_dir: Path,
    model: str,
    *,
    effective_cwd: Path | None = None,
    result_status: str = "SUCCESS",
    response: str = "AGY_PREFLIGHT_OK",
    conversation_id: str = "fresh-preflight-conversation",
    result_conversation_id: str | None = None,
    duplicate_result: bool = False,
    tools: list[object] | None = None,
    permission_mode: str = "always-proceed",
    num_turns: int = 1,
    duration_seconds: object = 0.1,
    drop_result_keys: tuple[str, ...] = (),
) -> str:
    cwd = effective_cwd or probe_dir
    result_payload = {
        "conversation_id": result_conversation_id or conversation_id,
        "status": result_status,
        "response": response,
        "duration_seconds": duration_seconds,
        "num_turns": num_turns,
        "usage": {},
    }
    for key in drop_result_keys:
        result_payload.pop(key, None)
    result = {
        "event": "result",
        "result": result_payload,
    }
    events = [
        {
            "event": "init",
            "conversation_id": conversation_id,
            "init": {
                "model": model,
                "cwd": str(cwd),
                "tools": tools if tools is not None else ["terminal"],
                "permission_mode": permission_mode,
            },
        },
        {
            "event": "step_update",
            "step_update": {
                "conversation_id": conversation_id,
                "step_index": 0,
                "state": "DONE",
                "step_type": "agent_response",
            },
        },
        result,
    ]
    if duplicate_result:
        events.append(result)
    return "\n".join(json.dumps(event) for event in events) + "\n"


def fixture_probe_runner(
    path: Path,
    config: Config,
    probe_dir: Path,
) -> dict[str, object]:
    prompt = "Reply with exactly AGY_PREFLIGHT_OK. Do not call tools and do not read or write any files."
    result = factory_module.command(
        [
            str(path),
            "--model",
            config.builder_model,
            "--dangerously-skip-permissions",
            "--output-format",
            "stream-json",
            "--disable-slash-commands",
            "--print-timeout",
            "90s",
            "--print",
            prompt,
        ],
        cwd=probe_dir,
        timeout=min(max(config.builder_timeout, 1), 120),
    )
    return {
        "returncode": result.returncode,
        "stdout": result.stdout or "",
        "stderr": result.stderr or "",
    }


class AgyResolutionTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self) -> None:
        self.temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.root = Path(self.temp.name)

    def tearDown(self) -> None:
        self.temp.cleanup()

    def executable(self, name: str = "agy.exe") -> Path:
        path = self.root / name
        path.write_bytes(b"native agy fixture")
        return path

    def test_explicit_path_wins_and_invalid_explicit_path_does_not_fall_back(self):
        explicit = self.executable()
        with patch("hermes_factory_v2.shutil.which", return_value=str(self.executable("path-agy.exe"))):
            self.assertEqual(resolve_agy_executable(str(explicit)), explicit.resolve())

        with patch("hermes_factory_v2.shutil.which", return_value=str(explicit)):
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(FactoryError, "not a valid file"):
                resolve_agy_executable(str(self.root / "missing.exe"))

    def test_path_then_windows_local_app_data_fallback(self):
        path_candidate = self.executable("path-agy.exe")
        with patch("hermes_factory_v2.shutil.which", return_value=str(path_candidate)):
            self.assertEqual(resolve_agy_executable(None), path_candidate.resolve())

        fallback = self.root / "agy" / "bin" / "agy.exe"
        fallback.parent.mkdir(parents=True)
        fallback.write_bytes(b"fallback agy fixture")
        with patch("hermes_factory_v2.shutil.which", return_value=None), patch(
            "hermes_factory_v2.os.name", "nt"
        ), patch.dict(os.environ, {"LOCALAPPDATA": str(self.root)}, clear=False):
            self.assertEqual(resolve_agy_executable(None), fallback.resolve())

    def test_directories_and_shell_shims_are_rejected(self):
        directory = self.root / "agy.exe"
        directory.mkdir()
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(FactoryError, "not a file"):
            resolve_agy_executable(str(directory))

        shim = self.executable("agy.cmd")
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(FactoryError, "unsupported shell shim"):
            resolve_agy_executable(str(shim))

        non_native = self.executable("agy.md")
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(FactoryError, "native Windows executable"):
            resolve_agy_executable(str(non_native))

    def test_windows_fallback_requires_local_app_data(self):
        with patch("hermes_factory_v2.shutil.which", return_value=None), patch(
            "hermes_factory_v2.os.name", "nt"
        ), patch.dict(os.environ, {"LOCALAPPDATA": ""}, clear=False):
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(FactoryError, "LOCALAPPDATA"):
                resolve_agy_executable(None)

    def test_factory_default_builder_is_direct_agy_after_step7(self):
        engine = FactoryEngine(state_root=self.root / "state", role_client=object(), builder=None)
        self.assertIsInstance(engine.builder, AgyBuilder)
        self.assertTrue(callable(getattr(engine.builder, "run", None)))


class AgyPreflightTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self) -> None:
        self.temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.root = Path(self.temp.name)
        self.run_dir = self.root / "run"
        self.run_dir.mkdir()
        self.executable = self.root / "agy.exe"
        self.executable.write_bytes(b"native agy fixture")
        self.failure_number = 0

    def tearDown(self) -> None:
        self.temp.cleanup()

    def config(self, **updates: object) -> Config:
        values: dict[str, object] = {
            "repo": str(self.root),
            "request": "preflight fixture",
            "requested_files": [],
            "test_command": None,
            "agy_bin": str(self.executable),
        }
        values.update(updates)
        return Config(**values)

    def run_preflight(
        self,
        fixture: CommandFixture,
        *,
        run_dir: Path | None = None,
        **config_updates: object,
    ) -> dict[str, object]:
        builder = AgyBuilder(str(self.executable), _probe_runner=fixture_probe_runner)
        approved_hash = hashlib.sha256(self.executable.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
        with patch("hermes_factory_v2.APPROVED_AGY_SHA256", approved_hash), patch(
            "hermes_factory_v2.command", side_effect=fixture
        ):
            return builder.preflight(self.config(**config_updates), run_dir or self.run_dir)

    def assert_preflight_rejected(self, fixture: CommandFixture, pattern: str) -> None:
        self.failure_number += 1
        failure_run_dir = self.root / f"failure-{self.failure_number}"
        failure_run_dir.mkdir()
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(FactoryError, pattern) as context:
            self.run_preflight(fixture, run_dir=failure_run_dir)
        self.assertEqual(context.exception.category, "preflight")
        failure_evidence = failure_run_dir / "preflight_builder.json"
        self.assertTrue(failure_evidence.exists())
        stored = json.loads(failure_evidence.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        self.assertEqual(stored["status"], "FAIL")
        self.assertEqual(stored["error_category"], "preflight")
        self.assertNotIn("stdout", stored)
        self.assertNotIn("stderr", stored)
        self.assertNotIn("GEMINI_API_KEY", json.dumps(stored))

    def test_success_records_pinned_identity_catalog_capabilities_and_probe(self):
        fixture = CommandFixture(model="gemini-3.8-flash-high")
        evidence = self.run_preflight(fixture)
        stored = json.loads((self.run_dir / "preflight_builder.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        expected_hash = hashlib.sha256(self.executable.read_bytes()).hexdigest()

        self.assertEqual(evidence, stored)
        self.assertEqual(stored["executable"]["path"], str(self.executable.resolve()))
        self.assertEqual(stored["executable"]["version"], "1.2.0")
        self.assertEqual(stored["executable"]["sha256"], expected_hash)
        self.assertEqual(stored["model_catalog"]["requested_model"], "gemini-3.8-flash-high")
        self.assertEqual(stored["model_catalog"]["exact_entry"], "Gemini 3.8 Flash (High)")
        self.assertEqual(stored["capabilities"]["missing"], [])
        self.assertEqual(stored["probe"]["status"], "SUCCESS")
        self.assertEqual(stored["probe"]["effective_model"], "gemini-3.8-flash-high")
        self.assertEqual(stored["probe"]["disposable_files_created"], [])
        self.assertNotIn("GEMINI_API_KEY", json.dumps(stored))
        self.assertNotIn("sk-", json.dumps(stored))

        probe_argv, probe_kwargs = fixture.calls[-1]
        self.assertEqual(probe_argv[1:], [
            "--model",
            "gemini-3.8-flash-high",
            "--dangerously-skip-permissions",
            "--output-format",
            "stream-json",
            "--disable-slash-commands",
            "--print-timeout",
            "90s",
            "--print",
            "Reply with exactly AGY_PREFLIGHT_OK. Do not call tools and do not read or write any files.",
        ])
        self.assertIsInstance(probe_kwargs["cwd"], Path)
        self.assertNotIn("--continue", probe_argv)
        self.assertNotIn("--conversation", probe_argv)
        self.assertNotIn("--project", probe_argv)

    def test_bannered_or_unsupported_versions_fail_closed(self):
        self.assert_preflight_rejected(
            CommandFixture(model="gemini-3.8-flash-high", version="agy version 1.2.0"),
            "bare semantic version",
        )
        self.assert_preflight_rejected(
            CommandFixture(model="gemini-3.8-flash-high", version="1.2.1"),
            "unsupported AGY version",
        )

    def test_missing_capability_fails_before_model_or_probe(self):
        help_text = HELP_TEXT.replace("--print-timeout", "")
        fixture = CommandFixture(model="gemini-3.8-flash-high", help_text=help_text)
        self.assert_preflight_rejected(fixture, "missing required capabilities")
        self.assertEqual(len(fixture.calls), 2)

    def test_help_parser_rejects_false_positive_tokens_and_wrong_output_format(self):
        embedded_only = "\n".join(
            f"prefix{capability}suffix" for capability in AGY_REQUIRED_CAPABILITIES
        ) + "\nprefix--output-formatsuffix stream-json"
        self.assert_preflight_rejected(
            CommandFixture(model="gemini-3.8-flash-high", help_text=embedded_only),
            "no parseable option declarations",
        )
        self.assert_preflight_rejected(
            CommandFixture(
                model="gemini-3.8-flash-high",
                help_text=HELP_TEXT.replace("stream-json", "ndjson"),
            ),
            "output-format=stream-json",
        )

    def test_malformed_or_missing_model_catalog_fails_closed(self):
        self.assert_preflight_rejected(
            CommandFixture(model="gemini-3.8-flash-high", models_text="not a catalog\n"),
            "malformed entry",
        )
        self.assert_preflight_rejected(
            CommandFixture(
                model="gemini-3.8-flash-high",
                models_text="gemini-3.1-pro-high\tGemini 3.1 Pro (High)\n",
            ),
            "does not contain",
        )

    def test_probe_nonzero_exit_and_error_result_fail_closed(self):
        self.assert_preflight_rejected(
            CommandFixture(model="gemini-3.8-flash-high", probe_returncode=1, probe_stderr="login required"),
            "capability probe failed",
        )
        def error_runner(
            path: Path,
            config: Config,
            probe_dir: Path,
        ) -> dict[str, object]:
            return {
                "returncode": 0,
                "stdout": make_probe_stream(
                    probe_dir,
                    config.builder_model,
                    result_status="ERROR",
                ),
                "stderr": "",
            }

        fixture = CommandFixture(model="gemini-3.8-flash-high")
        approved_hash = hashlib.sha256(self.executable.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
        with patch("hermes_factory_v2.APPROVED_AGY_SHA256", approved_hash), patch(
            "hermes_factory_v2.command", side_effect=fixture
        ):
            with self.assertRaisesRegex(FactoryError, "did not complete successfully"):
                AgyBuilder(
                    str(self.executable),
                    _probe_runner=error_runner,
                ).preflight(self.config(), self.run_dir)

    def test_unauthenticated_preflight_does_not_launch_a_browser(self):
        source = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "lib" / "hermes_factory_v2.py"
        source_text = source.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        self.assertNotIn("webbrowser", source_text)
        self.assertNotIn("start https", source_text.lower())

    def test_wrong_model_cwd_conversation_and_duplicate_result_fail_closed(self):
        self.assert_preflight_rejected(
            CommandFixture(model="gemini-3.8-flash-high", probe_model="gemini-3.7-flash-high"),
            "wrong model",
        )
        self.assert_preflight_rejected(
            CommandFixture(model="gemini-3.8-flash-high", probe_effective_cwd=self.root / "wrong-cwd"),
            "wrong cwd",
        )
        self.assert_preflight_rejected(
            CommandFixture(
                model="gemini-3.8-flash-high",
                probe_result_conversation_id="stale-conversation",
            ),
            "stale conversation",
        )
        self.assert_preflight_rejected(
            CommandFixture(model="gemini-3.8-flash-high", probe_duplicate_result=True),
            "exactly one terminal result",
        )

    def test_probe_requires_init_first_and_result_last(self):
        self.assert_preflight_rejected(
            CommandFixture(
                model="gemini-3.8-flash-high",
                probe_order="result_before_init",
            ),
            "begin with init",
        )
        self.assert_preflight_rejected(
            CommandFixture(
                model="gemini-3.8-flash-high",
                probe_order="event_after_result",
            ),
            "result must be the final event",
        )

    def test_probe_requires_audited_field_types_and_completion_metadata(self):
        invalid_fixtures = [
            (
                CommandFixture(model="gemini-3.8-flash-high", probe_tools=[123]),
                "missing audited capability metadata",
            ),
            (
                CommandFixture(
                    model="gemini-3.8-flash-high",
                    probe_permission_mode="request-review",
                ),
                "unexpected permission mode",
            ),
            (
                CommandFixture(
                    model="gemini-3.8-flash-high",
                    probe_drop_result_keys=("usage",),
                ),
                "missing required fields",
            ),
            (
                CommandFixture(model="gemini-3.8-flash-high", probe_num_turns=0),
                "invalid completion metadata",
            ),
            (
                CommandFixture(
                    model="gemini-3.8-flash-high",
                    probe_duration_seconds="unknown",
                ),
                "invalid completion metadata",
            ),
        ]
        for fixture, pattern in invalid_fixtures:
            with self.subTest(pattern=pattern):
                self.assert_preflight_rejected(fixture, pattern)

    def test_probe_filesystem_mutation_fails_before_success_evidence(self):
        self.assert_preflight_rejected(
            CommandFixture(model="gemini-3.8-flash-high", probe_create_file=True),
            "mutated its disposable boundary",
        )
        self.assert_preflight_rejected(
            CommandFixture(model="gemini-3.8-flash-high", probe_create_parent_file=True),
            "mutated its disposable boundary",
        )

    def test_malformed_probe_stream_fails_closed(self):
        fixture = CommandFixture(model="gemini-3.8-flash-high", probe_stdout="not json\n")
        self.assert_preflight_rejected(fixture, "malformed stream JSON")

    def test_binary_identity_change_after_probe_is_rejected(self):
        fixture = CommandFixture(model="gemini-3.8-flash-high")
        original = fixture.__call__

        def mutate_after_models(argv: list[str], **kwargs: object) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[str]:
            result = original(argv, **kwargs)
            if len(argv) > 1 and argv[1] == "models":
                self.executable.write_bytes(b"changed agy fixture")
            return result

        with self.assertRaisesRegex(FactoryError, "identity changed"):
            approved_hash = hashlib.sha256(self.executable.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
            with patch("hermes_factory_v2.APPROVED_AGY_SHA256", approved_hash), patch(
                "hermes_factory_v2.command", side_effect=mutate_after_models
            ):
                AgyBuilder(str(self.executable), _probe_runner=fixture_probe_runner).preflight(
                    self.config(), self.run_dir
                )
        self.assertEqual(
            [argv[1] for argv, _ in fixture.calls],
            ["--version", "--help", "models"],
        )

    def test_pinned_identity_survives_a_new_builder_instance(self):
        config = self.config(agy_bin=None)
        fixture = CommandFixture(model="gemini-3.8-flash-high")
        approved_hash = hashlib.sha256(self.executable.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
        with patch("hermes_factory_v2.APPROVED_AGY_SHA256", approved_hash), patch(
            "hermes_factory_v2.shutil.which", return_value=str(self.executable)
        ), patch("hermes_factory_v2.command", side_effect=fixture):
            AgyBuilder(_probe_runner=fixture_probe_runner).preflight(config, self.run_dir)

        self.executable.write_bytes(b"changed after first process")
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(FactoryError, "identity changed"):
            with patch("hermes_factory_v2.shutil.which", return_value=str(self.executable)), patch(
                "hermes_factory_v2.APPROVED_AGY_SHA256", approved_hash
            ):
                AgyBuilder(_probe_runner=fixture_probe_runner).preflight(config, self.run_dir)
        original_evidence = json.loads(
            (self.run_dir / "preflight_builder.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        )
        self.assertEqual(original_evidence["executable"]["sha256"], approved_hash)
        self.assertTrue((self.run_dir / "preflight_builder_failure.json").exists())

        with self.assertRaisesRegex(FactoryError, "since preflight_builder.json"):
            with patch("hermes_factory_v2.shutil.which", return_value=str(self.executable)), patch(
                "hermes_factory_v2.APPROVED_AGY_SHA256", approved_hash
            ):
                AgyBuilder(_probe_runner=fixture_probe_runner).preflight(config, self.run_dir)

    def test_same_version_but_unapproved_binary_is_rejected_before_invocation(self):
        fixture = CommandFixture(model="gemini-3.8-flash-high")
        failure_run_dir = self.root / "unapproved"
        failure_run_dir.mkdir()
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(FactoryError, "not an approved audited identity"):
            with patch("hermes_factory_v2.command", side_effect=fixture):
                AgyBuilder(
                    str(self.executable),
                    _probe_runner=fixture_probe_runner,
                ).preflight(self.config(), failure_run_dir)
        self.assertEqual(fixture.calls, [])

    def test_preflight_command_failures_are_preflight_and_persisted(self):
        def failing_command(argv: list[str], **kwargs: object) -> object:
            raise FactoryError("command timed out after 30s", "failed")

        approved_hash = hashlib.sha256(self.executable.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
        with self.assertRaisesRegex(FactoryError, "command timed out"):
            with patch("hermes_factory_v2.APPROVED_AGY_SHA256", approved_hash), patch(
                "hermes_factory_v2.command", side_effect=failing_command
            ):
                AgyBuilder(str(self.executable), _probe_runner=fixture_probe_runner).preflight(
                    self.config(), self.run_dir
                )
        self.assertEqual(
            json.loads((self.run_dir / "preflight_builder.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))[
                "status"
            ],
            "FAIL",
        )

    def test_failure_artifact_omits_raw_secret_diagnostics(self):
        builder = AgyBuilder(str(self.executable), _probe_runner=fixture_probe_runner)
        builder._write_preflight_failure(
            self.run_dir,
            "GEMINI_API_KEY=fixture-secret Bearer bearer-secret oauth_code=oauth-secret Cookie: cookie-secret",
        )
        stored = json.loads((self.run_dir / "preflight_builder.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        serialized = json.dumps(stored)
        for secret in ("fixture-secret", "bearer-secret", "oauth-secret", "cookie-secret"):
            self.assertNotIn(secret, serialized)
        self.assertEqual(stored["error"], "preflight_failed")
        self.assertIn("failure_message_sha256", stored)

    def test_engine_persists_pinned_identity_in_run_state(self):
        executable = self.executable

        class Builder:
            def preflight(self, config: Config, run_dir: Path) -> dict[str, object]:
                return {
                    "executable": {
                        "path": str(executable.resolve()),
                        "sha256": "a" * 64,
                    }
                }

        class SmokeRoles:
            def call(self, *, role: str, **kwargs: object) -> dict[str, object]:
                if role == "Luna":
                    return {"capability": "debugger", "schema_ok": True}
                return {"capability": "planner-reviewer", "schema_ok": True}

        state = {"phase": "initializing"}
        atomic_json(self.run_dir / "run_state.json", state)
        engine = FactoryEngine(
            state_root=self.root / "state",
            role_client=SmokeRoles(),
            builder=Builder(),
        )
        engine._capability_preflight(self.run_dir, state, self.config())
        persisted = json.loads((self.run_dir / "run_state.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        self.assertEqual(persisted["agy_executable_path"], str(self.executable.resolve()))
        self.assertEqual(persisted["agy_executable_sha256"], "a" * 64)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    unittest.main()
"""Step 7 tests for the direct AGY Builder and stream protocol."""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import os
from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
import tempfile
import threading
import time
import unittest
from unittest.mock import patch

import sys

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent / "lib"))
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent))

import hermes_factory_v2 as factory_module  # noqa: E402
from hermes_factory_v2 import (  # noqa: E402
    AGY_EVENT_RECORD_MAX_BYTES,
    AgyBuilder,
    BuilderFailure,
    BuilderProcessError,
    Config,
    FactoryError,
    parse_agy_event_stream,
)


FIXTURE_METADATA = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "fixtures" / "agy" / "1.2.0" / "step7_stream_fixtures.json"
PROCESS_FIXTURE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent / "fixtures" / "builder_process_fixture.py"


def make_stream(
    *,
    model: str,
    cwd: str,
    conversation_id: str = "fresh-conversation",
    result_conversation_id: str | None = None,
    status: str = "SUCCESS",
    response: str = "implementation complete; error and token limit are ordinary prose",
    num_turns: int = 1,
    duplicate_result: bool = False,
    omit_result: bool = False,
    omit_update: bool = False,
    duplicate_key: bool = False,
    extra_init: bool = False,
) -> str:
    init = {
        "event": "init",
        "conversation_id": conversation_id,
        "init": {
            "model": model,
            "cwd": cwd,
            "tools": ["terminal", "file"],
            "permission_mode": "always-proceed",
        },
    }
    update = {
        "event": "step_update",
        "step_update": {
            "conversation_id": conversation_id,
            "step_index": 0,
            "state": "DONE",
            "step_type": "agent_response",
        },
    }
    result_payload: dict[str, object] = {
        "conversation_id": result_conversation_id or conversation_id,
        "status": status,
        "response": response,
        "duration_seconds": 0.2,
        "num_turns": num_turns,
        "usage": {"input_tokens": 1, "output_tokens": 1},
    }
    if status == "ERROR":
        result_payload["error"] = "fixture runtime error"
    result = {"event": "result", "result": result_payload}
    events = [init] if omit_update else [init, update]
    if extra_init:
        events.append(
            {
                "event": "init",
                "conversation_id": "later-conversation",
                "init": {
                    "model": model,
                    "cwd": cwd,
                    "tools": ["terminal", "file"],
                    "permission_mode": "always-proceed",
                },
            }
        )
    if not omit_result:
        events.append(result)
        if duplicate_result:
            events.append(result)
    if duplicate_key:
        return (
            '{"event":"init","event":"init","conversation_id":"fresh-conversation",'
            '"init":{"model":"gemini-3.8-flash-high","cwd":"%s",'
            '"tools":["terminal"],"permission_mode":"always-proceed"}}\n' % cwd
        )
    return "\n".join(json.dumps(event, ensure_ascii=False) for event in events) + "\n"


class AgyBuilderRunTests(unittest.TestCase):
    def setUp(self) -> None:
        self.temp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
        self.root = Path(self.temp.name)
        self.source = self.root / "source"
        self.candidate = self.root / "candidate"
        for repo in (self.source, self.candidate):
            repo.mkdir()
            subprocess.run(["git", "init", "-q"], cwd=repo, check=True)
            subprocess.run(["git", "config", "user.name", "Step 7 Test"], cwd=repo, check=True)
            subprocess.run(["git", "config", "user.email", "step7@example.invalid"], cwd=repo, check=True)
        (self.source / "allowed.py").write_text("value = 1\n", encoding="utf-8")
        (self.candidate / "allowed.py").write_text("value = 1\n", encoding="utf-8")
        for repo in (self.source, self.candidate):
            subprocess.run(["git", "add", "allowed.py"], cwd=repo, check=True)
            subprocess.run(["git", "commit", "-qm", "fixture"], cwd=repo, check=True)
        self.executable = self.root / "agy.exe"
        self.executable.write_bytes(b"audited agy fixture")
        self.executable_hash = hashlib.sha256(self.executable.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
        self.run_dir = self.root / "run"
        self.config = Config(
            repo=str(self.source),
            request="step 7 fixture",
            requested_files=["allowed.py"],
            test_command="python -c pass",
            agy_bin=str(self.executable),
            builder_timeout=30,
        )
        self.calls: list[dict[str, object]] = []

    def tearDown(self) -> None:
        self.temp.cleanup()

    def fake_process(self, *, stream: str, returncode: int = 0, stderr: str = ""):
        def call(argv, **kwargs):
            self.calls.append({"argv": list(argv), **kwargs})
            Path(kwargs["stdout_path"]).write_bytes(stream.encode("utf-8"))
            Path(kwargs["stderr_path"]).write_bytes(stderr.encode("utf-8"))
            return subprocess.CompletedProcess(argv, returncode, stream, stderr)

        return call

    def run_builder(self, stream: str, *, returncode: int = 0, stderr: str = "") -> AgyBuilder:
        builder = AgyBuilder(str(self.executable))
        with patch.object(factory_module, "APPROVED_AGY_SHA256", self.executable_hash), patch.object(
            factory_module,
            "run_builder_process",
            side_effect=self.fake_process(stream=stream, returncode=returncode, stderr=stderr),
        ):
            builder.run(
                worktree=self.candidate,
                instruction="Make the allowed change; do not use Git.",
                allowed_files=["allowed.py"],
                config=self.config,
                run_dir=self.run_dir,
                label="builder_initial",
            )
        return builder

    def stream(self, **updates: object) -> str:
        values: dict[str, object] = {
            "model": self.config.builder_model,
            "cwd": str(self.candidate.resolve()),
        }
        values.update(updates)
        return make_stream(**values)

    def test_valid_run_binds_attempt_artifact_and_argv(self) -> None:
        self.run_builder(self.stream())
        self.assertEqual(len(self.calls), 1)
        call = self.calls[0]
        argv = call["argv"]
        self.assertEqual(argv[0], str(self.executable.resolve()))
        self.assertEqual(argv[1:9], [
            "--model",
            "gemini-3.8-flash-high",
            "--dangerously-skip-permissions",
            "--output-format",
            "stream-json",
            "--disable-slash-commands",
            "--print-timeout",
            "24s",
        ])
        self.assertEqual(argv[9], "--print")
        self.assertNotIn("--continue", argv)
        self.assertNotIn("--conversation", argv)
        self.assertEqual(call["cwd"], self.candidate.resolve())
        attempts = list((self.run_dir / "builder_attempts").glob("builder_initial-*"))
        self.assertEqual(len(attempts), 1)
        artifact = json.loads((attempts[0] / "attempt.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        self.assertEqual(artifact["status"], "SUCCESS")
        self.assertEqual(artifact["allowed_files"], ["allowed.py"])
        self.assertEqual(artifact["executable"]["sha256"], self.executable_hash)
        self.assertTrue((attempts[0] / "instruction.txt").is_file())
        self.assertFalse((self.candidate / "builder_attempts").exists())

    def test_fixture_metadata_is_sanitized_and_complete(self) -> None:
        data = json.loads(FIXTURE_METADATA.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        self.assertTrue(data["sanitized"])
        self.assertIn("missing_terminal_result", data["negative_cases"])
        self.assertIn("duplicate_json_key", data["negative_cases"])

    def test_parser_accepts_ordinary_response_words(self) -> None:
        parsed = parse_agy_event_stream(self.stream().encode("utf-8"))
        self.assertEqual(parsed["result"]["status"], "SUCCESS")
        self.assertIn("token limit", parsed["result"]["response"])

    def test_parser_rejects_required_negative_cases(self) -> None:
        cases = [
            (b"\xff", "agy_protocol_error"),
            (self.stream(omit_result=True).encode("utf-8"), "agy_output_incomplete"),
            (make_stream(model=self.config.builder_model, cwd=str(self.candidate), duplicate_result=True).encode("utf-8"), "agy_protocol_error"),
            (make_stream(model=self.config.builder_model, cwd=str(self.candidate), duplicate_key=True).encode("utf-8"), "agy_protocol_error"),
            (make_stream(model=self.config.builder_model, cwd=str(self.candidate), status="ERROR").encode("utf-8"), "agy_runtime_error"),
            (make_stream(model=self.config.builder_model, cwd=str(self.candidate), num_turns=0).encode("utf-8"), "agy_output_incomplete"),
        ]
        for raw, code in cases:
            with self.subTest(code=code):
                with self.assertRaises(BuilderFailure) as context:
                    parse_agy_event_stream(raw)
                self.assertEqual(context.exception.code, code)

    def test_parser_requires_a_step_update_before_terminal_success(self) -> None:
        with self.assertRaises(BuilderFailure) as context:
            parse_agy_event_stream(
                self.stream(omit_update=True).encode("utf-8")
            )
        self.assertEqual(context.exception.code, "agy_output_incomplete")

    def test_run_maps_wrong_model_cwd_and_process_failures(self) -> None:
        cases = [
            (self.stream(model="gemini-3.7-flash-high"), "agy_model_mismatch", "model"),
            (self.stream(cwd=str(self.source.resolve()), conversation_id="wrong-cwd"), "agy_isolation_failed", "cwd"),
        ]
        for stream, code, label in cases:
            with self.subTest(label=label):
                builder = AgyBuilder(str(self.executable))
                with patch.object(factory_module, "APPROVED_AGY_SHA256", self.executable_hash), patch.object(
                    factory_module,
                    "run_builder_process",
                    side_effect=self.fake_process(stream=stream),
                ):
                    with self.assertRaises(BuilderFailure) as context:
                        builder.run(
                            worktree=self.candidate,
                            instruction="bounded instruction",
                            allowed_files=["allowed.py"],
                            config=self.config,
                            run_dir=self.run_dir,
                            label="builder_retry",
                        )
                self.assertEqual(context.exception.code, code)

        builder = AgyBuilder(str(self.executable))
        process_error = BuilderProcessError(
            "Builder process timed out; partial evidence was retained",
            stdout_path=self.root / "stdout",
            stderr_path=self.root / "stderr",
            failure_code="agy_timeout",
        )
        with patch.object(factory_module, "APPROVED_AGY_SHA256", self.executable_hash), patch.object(
            factory_module, "run_builder_process", side_effect=process_error
        ):
            with self.assertRaises(BuilderFailure) as context:
                builder.run(
                    worktree=self.candidate,
                    instruction="bounded instruction",
                    allowed_files=["allowed.py"],
                    config=self.config,
                    run_dir=self.run_dir,
                    label="builder_timeout",
                )
        self.assertEqual(context.exception.code, "agy_timeout")

    def test_run_rejects_stale_conversation_and_nonzero_exit(self) -> None:
        stream = self.stream(conversation_id="same-id")
        builder = AgyBuilder(str(self.executable))
        with patch.object(factory_module, "APPROVED_AGY_SHA256", self.executable_hash), patch.object(
            factory_module, "run_builder_process", side_effect=self.fake_process(stream=stream)
        ):
            builder.run(
                worktree=self.candidate,
                instruction="first bounded instruction",
                allowed_files=["allowed.py"],
                config=self.config,
                run_dir=self.run_dir,
                label="first",
            )
            with self.assertRaises(BuilderFailure) as context:
                builder.run(
                    worktree=self.candidate,
                    instruction="second bounded instruction",
                    allowed_files=["allowed.py"],
                    config=self.config,
                    run_dir=self.run_dir,
                    label="second",
                )
        self.assertEqual(context.exception.code, "agy_protocol_error")

        builder = AgyBuilder(str(self.executable))
        nonzero_stream = self.stream(conversation_id="nonzero-id")
        with patch.object(factory_module, "APPROVED_AGY_SHA256", self.executable_hash), patch.object(
            factory_module,
            "run_builder_process",
            side_effect=self.fake_process(stream=nonzero_stream, returncode=1),
        ):
            with self.assertRaises(BuilderFailure) as context:
                builder.run(
                    worktree=self.candidate,
                    instruction="failed bounded instruction",
                    allowed_files=["allowed.py"],
                    config=self.config,
                    run_dir=self.run_dir,
                    label="nonzero",
                )
        self.assertEqual(context.exception.code, "agy_runtime_error")

    def test_failed_conversation_is_reserved_across_attempts_and_instances(self) -> None:
        stream = self.stream(conversation_id="reserved-error", status="ERROR")
        first = AgyBuilder(str(self.executable))
        with patch.object(factory_module, "APPROVED_AGY_SHA256", self.executable_hash), patch.object(
            factory_module, "run_builder_process", side_effect=self.fake_process(stream=stream)
        ):
            with self.assertRaises(BuilderFailure) as first_context:
                first.run(
                    worktree=self.candidate,
                    instruction="failed first turn",
                    allowed_files=["allowed.py"],
                    config=self.config,
                    run_dir=self.run_dir,
                    label="error_first",
                )
        self.assertEqual(first_context.exception.code, "agy_runtime_error")
        attempt_files = list((self.run_dir / "builder_attempts").glob("error_first-*/attempt.json"))
        self.assertEqual(len(attempt_files), 1)
        first_artifact = json.loads(attempt_files[0].read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        self.assertRegex(first_artifact["conversation_id_sha256"], r"^[0-9a-f]{64}$")
        self.assertEqual(first_artifact["conversation_reservation"], "RESERVED")

        second = AgyBuilder(str(self.executable))
        with patch.object(factory_module, "APPROVED_AGY_SHA256", self.executable_hash), patch.object(
            factory_module, "run_builder_process", side_effect=self.fake_process(stream=stream)
        ):
            with self.assertRaises(BuilderFailure) as second_context:
                second.run(
                    worktree=self.candidate,
                    instruction="reused failed turn",
                    allowed_files=["allowed.py"],
                    config=self.config,
                    run_dir=self.run_dir,
                    label="error_second",
                )
        self.assertEqual(second_context.exception.code, "agy_protocol_error")

    def test_all_init_identities_are_reserved_before_malformed_stream_acceptance(self) -> None:
        stream = self.stream(conversation_id="first-conversation", extra_init=True)
        builder = AgyBuilder(str(self.executable))
        with patch.object(factory_module, "APPROVED_AGY_SHA256", self.executable_hash), patch.object(
            factory_module, "run_builder_process", side_effect=self.fake_process(stream=stream)
        ):
            with self.assertRaises(BuilderFailure) as context:
                builder.run(
                    worktree=self.candidate,
                    instruction="duplicate init stream",
                    allowed_files=["allowed.py"],
                    config=self.config,
                    run_dir=self.run_dir,
                    label="duplicate_init",
                )
        self.assertEqual(context.exception.code, "agy_protocol_error")
        evidence_path = next((self.run_dir / "builder_attempts").glob("duplicate_init-*/attempt.json"))
        evidence = json.loads(evidence_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        self.assertEqual(len(evidence["conversation_id_sha256s"]), 2)

    def test_corrupt_prior_reservation_evidence_fails_closed(self) -> None:
        corrupt = self.run_dir / "builder_attempts" / "corrupt"
        corrupt.mkdir(parents=True)
        (corrupt / "attempt.json").write_text("not-json", encoding="utf-8")
        builder = AgyBuilder(str(self.executable))
        with patch.object(factory_module, "APPROVED_AGY_SHA256", self.executable_hash), patch.object(
            factory_module, "run_builder_process", side_effect=self.fake_process(stream=self.stream())
        ):
            with self.assertRaises(BuilderFailure) as context:
                builder.run(
                    worktree=self.candidate,
                    instruction="corrupt reservation",
                    allowed_files=["allowed.py"],
                    config=self.config,
                    run_dir=self.run_dir,
                    label="corrupt_reservation",
                )
        self.assertEqual(context.exception.code, "agy_protocol_error")

    def test_all_supervisor_failure_classes_map_without_raw_diagnostics(self) -> None:
        cases = [
            ("spawn failed", "agy_spawn_failed"),
            ("cancelled", "agy_canceled"),
            ("output exceeded bounded evidence limit", "agy_output_incomplete"),
            ("malformed UTF-8", "agy_output_incomplete"),
            ("process boundary did not become quiescent", "agy_isolation_failed"),
            ("generic process failure", "agy_runtime_error"),
        ]
        for index, (message, code) in enumerate(cases):
            with self.subTest(code=code):
                builder = AgyBuilder(str(self.executable))
                process_error = BuilderProcessError(
                    f"{message}; secret-prompt-value",
                    stdout_path=self.root / f"stdout-{index}",
                    stderr_path=self.root / f"stderr-{index}",
                    failure_code=code,
                )
                with patch.object(factory_module, "APPROVED_AGY_SHA256", self.executable_hash), patch.object(
                    factory_module, "run_builder_process", side_effect=process_error
                ):
                    with self.assertRaises(BuilderFailure) as context:
                        builder.run(
                            worktree=self.candidate,
                            instruction="secret-prompt-value",
                            allowed_files=["allowed.py"],
                            config=self.config,
                            run_dir=self.run_dir,
                            label=f"process_{index}",
                        )
                self.assertEqual(context.exception.code, code)
                self.assertNotIn("secret-prompt-value", str(context.exception))

    def test_initial_process_evidence_write_failure_is_structured(self) -> None:
        builder = AgyBuilder(str(self.executable))
        with patch.object(factory_module, "APPROVED_AGY_SHA256", self.executable_hash), patch.object(
            factory_module,
            "_write_builder_evidence",
